(a_{n})_{n}=[(a_{n\varepsilon})_{n,\varepsilon}]=[a_{n\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}
.
Note that in the term
a_{n\varepsilon}
, we have
n\in\mathbb{N}
, and not
n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
.
Similarly, we can define the ring
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}{}_{\mathrm{s}}:=\left(\mathbb{R}^{\mathbb{N}\times I}\right)_{\rho}/\penalty\sim_{\rho}
.

For some results, such as e.g. the ratio test Thm.
23

and the root test Thm.
24
(where we need to perform
term by term operations in a hyperseries) the following
uniform

notions or moderateness will be useful:

\displaystyle(a_{n\varepsilon})_{n,\varepsilon}\in\left(\mathbb{C}^{\mathbb{N}\times I}\right)_{\mathrm{u},\rho}\quad

\displaystyle:\iff\quad\forall Q\in\mathbb{N}\,\forall^{0}\varepsilon\,\forall n\in\mathbb{N}:\ |a_{n\varepsilon}|\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-Q},

\displaystyle(a_{n\varepsilon})_{n,\varepsilon}\sim_{\mathrm{u},\rho}(\bar{a}_{n\varepsilon})_{n,\varepsilon}\quad

\displaystyle:\iff\quad\forall q\in\mathbb{N}\,\forall^{0}\varepsilon\,\forall n\in\mathbb{N}:\ |a_{n\varepsilon}-\bar{a}_{n\varepsilon}|\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{q}.

The corresponding quotient ring is denoted by
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{\mathrm{u}}:=\left(\mathbb{C}^{\mathbb{N}\times I}\right)_{\mathrm{u},\rho}/\sim_{\mathrm{u},\rho}

and the equivalence classes by
[a_{n\varepsilon}]_{\mathrm{u}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{\mathrm{u}}
.
Similarly, we can define the ring
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}{}_{\mathrm{u}}:=\left(\mathbb{R}^{\mathbb{N}\times I}\right)_{\mathrm{u},\rho}/\penalty\sim_{\mathrm{u},\rho}
.

Clearly, the adjective
uniform
refers to the logical
structure
\exists\varepsilon_{0}\,\forall\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}\,\forall n\in\mathbb{N}
.
Moreover, it is easy to prove that both
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}\subseteq\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}

and
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}\subseteq\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm u}}
via the embedding

[x_{\varepsilon}]\mapsto[(x_{\varepsilon})_{n,\varepsilon}]_{\mathrm{s}(u)}
, and that
the map
\lambda:[a_{n\varepsilon}]_{\mathrm{u}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{\mathrm{u}}\mapsto[a_{n\varepsilon}]_{\mathrm{s}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{\mathrm{s}}

is well-defined and linear. On the other hand, the possible injectivity
of
\lambda
is still and open problem.

Definition 13
.

Let
(a_{n})_{n}=[a_{n\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}
,
then the term

\sum_{n=N}^{M}a_{n}:=\left[\sum_{n={\rm ni}(N)_{\varepsilon}}^{{\rm ni}(M)_{\varepsilon}}a_{n\varepsilon}\right]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}\quad\forall N,M\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}

(2.9)

is called
\rho
-hypersum
of
(a_{n})_{n}
. Moreover, we
say that
s
is the
\rho
-sum of the hyperseries with terms

(a_{n})_{n}
as

n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
if the hypersequence
N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\longmapsto\sum_{n=0}^{N}a_{n}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}

converges to
s\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
. In this case, we write

s=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\sum_{n=0}^{N}a_{n}=:\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}.

(2.10)

As usual, we also say that the hyperseries
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}

is convergent
if

\exists\,\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$ Whereas, we say that a hyperseries
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}

does
not converge
if
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\sum_{n=0}^{N}a_{n}
does not
exist in
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
. More specifically, if
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\sum_{n=0}^{N}a_{n}=+\infty\,(-\infty)
,
we say that
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\sum_{n=0}^{N}a_{n}

diverges

to
+\infty

(-\infty)
.

Note that if
N
,
M\in\mathbb{N}
, then
\sum_{n=N}^{M}a_{n}=a_{N}+\dots+a_{M}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}

is the usual finite sum. In particular, we have the following properties:

Lemma 14
.

If
N=[N_{\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
, with
N_{\varepsilon}\in\mathbb{N}
,
and
(a_{n})_{n}=[a_{n\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}
, then
a_{N}:=\left[a_{N_{\varepsilon},\varepsilon}\right]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}

is well defined. That is, any sequence
(a_{n})_{n}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}

can be extended from
\mathbb{N}
to the entire set
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
of hyperfinite
numbers.

Proof.

In fact, if
(N_{\varepsilon})\in\mathbb{N}_{\rho}
, then from
(a_{n})_{n}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}

we get the existence of
Q_{i}\in\mathbb{N}
such that

\left|a_{N_{\varepsilon},\varepsilon}\right|=\left|\sum_{n=0}^{N_{\varepsilon}}a_{n\varepsilon}-\sum_{n=0}^{N_{\varepsilon}-1}a_{n\varepsilon}\right|\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-Q_{1}}+\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-Q_{2}}.

Finally, if
(a_{n})_{n}=\left[a_{n\varepsilon}\right]=\left[\bar{a}_{n\varepsilon}\right]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}
,
then directly from (
2.8
) with
M=N
we get

\left[\left(a_{{\rm ni}(N)_{\varepsilon},\varepsilon}\right)_{n,\varepsilon}\right]=\left[\left(\bar{a}_{{\rm ni}(N)_{\varepsilon},\varepsilon}\right)_{n,\varepsilon}\right]
.
Therefore,
a_{N}:=[a_{N_{\varepsilon},\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
is well-defined.
In particular, this applies with
N=n\in\mathbb{N}
, so that any equivalence
class
(a_{n})_{n}=\left[\left(a_{n\varepsilon}\right)_{n,\varepsilon}\right]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}

also defines an ordinary sequence
\left(a_{n}\right)_{n\in\mathbb{N}}=\left(\left[a_{n\varepsilon}\right]\right)_{n\in\mathbb{N}}

of
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
. On the other hand, let us explicitly note that
if
\left(a_{n}\right)_{n\in\mathbb{N}}=\left(\bar{a}_{n}\right)_{n\in\mathbb{N}}
,
i.e. if
a_{n}=\bar{a}_{n}
for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
, then not necessarily
(
2.8
) holds, i.e. we can have
(a_{n})_{n}\neq(\bar{a}_{n})_{n}

as elements of the quotient module
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}
.
∎

Example 15
.

The following examples of convergent hyperseries
justify our definition of hyperseries by recovering classical examples
such as geometric and exponential complex hyperseries. We recall that
this is not possible using classical series in a non-Archimedean setting.

(i)

Let
N=[N_{\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
, where
N_{\varepsilon}\in\mathbb{N}
for all
\varepsilon
,
then
\sum_{n=N}^{N}a_{n}=\left[a_{N_{\varepsilon},\varepsilon}\right]=a_{N}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
.
We recall that
a_{N}=\left[a_{N_{\varepsilon},\varepsilon}\right]
is the extension
of
(a_{n})_{n}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}
(see Lem.
14
).

(ii)

For all
k\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
,
0<|k|<1
,
we have

\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}k^{n}=\frac{1}{1-k}.

(2.11)

We first note that
|k^{n}|\leq 1
for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
, so that
(k^{n})_{n}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}
.
Now,

\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}k^{n}=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\sum_{n=0}^{N}k^{n}=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\frac{1-k^{N+1}}{1-k}.

But
|k|^{N+1}<\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}^{q}
if and only if
(N+1)\log|k|<q\log\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}
.
Since
0<|k|<1
, we have
\log|k|<0
and we obtain
N>q\frac{\log d}{\log|k|}-1
.
It suffices to take
M_{\varepsilon}:={\rm int}\left(q\frac{\log\rho_{\varepsilon}}{\log|k|_{\varepsilon}}\right)

in the definition of hyperlimit.

(iii)

Let
k\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{>0}
such that
|k|>_{\text{\rm s}}1

then the hyperseries
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}k^{n}
is not convergent.
In fact, by contradiction, in the opposite case we would have
\sum_{n=0}^{N}k^{n}\in(l-1,l+1)

for some
l\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}
for all
N
sufficiently large, but this
is impossible because for all fixed
L\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{>0}
and for

N
sufficiently large,

\sum_{n=0}^{N}k^{n}=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\frac{1-k^{N+1}}{1-k}>_{\text{\rm s}}L,

because
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}k^{N+1}=_{s}+\infty
.

(iv)

For all
z\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
such that

|z|<-K\log\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}
, for some
K\in\mathbb{R}
, we have
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\frac{z^{n}}{n!}=e^{z}
.
Since
|z|<-K\log\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}
, we have
|z_{\varepsilon}|\leq-K\log\rho_{\varepsilon}

for
\varepsilon
small. Thereby,
\frac{|z_{\varepsilon}|^{n}}{n!}\leq e^{-K\log\rho_{\varepsilon}}=\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-K}

for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
and thus
\left(\frac{z^{n}}{n!}\right)_{n}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}
.
For all
N=[N_{\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
,
N_{\varepsilon}\in\mathbb{N}
, and all
\varepsilon
,
we have

\sum_{n=0}^{N_{\varepsilon}}\frac{z_{\varepsilon}^{n}}{n!}=e^{z_{\varepsilon}}-\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}+1}^{+\infty}\frac{z_{\varepsilon}^{n}}{n!}.

(2.12)

Now, take
N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
such that
\frac{-K\log\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}}{N+1}<\frac{1}{2}
,
so that we can assume
N_{\varepsilon}+1>2\left(\log\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-K}\right)

for all
\varepsilon
. We have
\left|\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}+1}^{+\infty}\frac{z_{\varepsilon}^{n}}{n!}\right|\leq\sum_{n>N_{\varepsilon}}\frac{\left(\log\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-K}\right)^{n}}{n!}
,
and for all
n\geq N_{\varepsilon}
, we have (by induction)

\frac{\left(\log\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-K}\right)^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}<\frac{1}{2^{n+1}}.

Therefore
\left|\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}+1}^{+\infty}\frac{z_{\varepsilon}^{n}}{n!}\right|\leq\sum_{n>N_{\varepsilon}}\frac{1}{2^{n}}

and hence
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\sum_{n=N+1}^{+\infty}\frac{z^{n}}{n!}=0

by (
2.11
). This and (
2.12
) yield
the conclusion.

The following result allows us to obtain hyperseries by considering

\varepsilon
-wise convergence of its summands. Its proof is clearly very
similar to that of Thm.
7
, but with a
special attention to the condition
(a_{n})_{n}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}
.

Theorem 16
.

Let
(a_{n,\varepsilon})_{n,\varepsilon}:\mathbb{N}\times I\longrightarrow\mathbb{C}
.
Assume that for all
\varepsilon
, the standard series
\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}a_{n,\varepsilon}

converges to
s_{\varepsilon}\in\mathbb{C}
, i.e.

\forall\varepsilon\,\forall q\in\mathbb{N}\,\exists M_{\varepsilon q}\in\mathbb{N}_{>0}\,\forall N\geq M_{\varepsilon q}:\left|\sum_{n=0}^{N}a_{n,\varepsilon}-s_{\varepsilon}\right|<\rho_{\varepsilon}^{q},

(2.13)

such that
l_{\varepsilon}\in\mathbb{C}
and (without loss of generality)

M_{\varepsilon q}\leq M_{\varepsilon q+1}\quad\forall\varepsilon\forall q.

(2.14)

Set
M_{\varepsilon}:=M_{\varepsilon q_{\varepsilon}^{-1}}^{-1}
. Then, if
\left(M_{\varepsilon}\right)\in\mathbb{R}_{\rho}
:

(i)

M_{q}:=[M_{\varepsilon q}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
for all
q\in\mathbb{N}
;

(ii)

If
(s_{\varepsilon})\in\mathbb{C}_{\rho}
then there exists
M\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
such
that

(i)

s_{M}:=\left[\sum_{n=0}^{M_{\varepsilon}}a_{n,\varepsilon}\right]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
;

(ii)

(a_{n+M})_{n}:=\left[\left(a_{n+M_{\varepsilon},\varepsilon}\right)_{n,\varepsilon}\right]_{s}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}
;

(iii)

s=s_{M}+\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\sigma$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\sigma$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{a_{n+M}}
.

Proof.

Since
\forall q\in\mathbb{N}\,\forall^{0}\varepsilon:q\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-1}
, (
2.14
)
implies

\forall q\in\mathbb{N}\,\forall^{0}\varepsilon:M_{\varepsilon}\geq M_{\varepsilon q}.

(2.15)

We can hence
M:=[M_{\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
,
M_{q}:=[M_{\varepsilon q}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}

because of the assumption
\left(M_{\varepsilon}\right)\in\mathbb{R}_{\rho}
and
of (
2.15
). Since for all
\varepsilon
condition (
2.13
)
yields
\left|\sum_{n=0}^{N}a_{n,\varepsilon}-s_{\varepsilon}\right|<\rho_{\varepsilon}^{q}<\sigma_{\varepsilon}^{q}<1

for all
N\geq M_{\varepsilon q}
and if
N=M
, this gives
s_{M}=\left[\sum_{n=0}^{M_{\varepsilon}}a_{n,\varepsilon}\right]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
,
because we assumed that
s:=\left[s_{\varepsilon}\right]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
.
If
N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
, then
\text{ni}(N)_{\varepsilon}+M_{\varepsilon}\geq M_{\varepsilon}

for all
\varepsilon
, and hence

\left|\sum_{n=0}^{M_{\varepsilon}-1}a_{n,\varepsilon}+\sum_{n=M_{\varepsilon}}^{{\rm ni}(N)_{\varepsilon}+M_{\varepsilon}}a_{n,\varepsilon}-s_{\varepsilon}\right|=\left|s_{M_{\varepsilon}}+\sum_{n=0}^{{\rm ni}(N)_{\varepsilon}}a_{n+M_{\varepsilon},\varepsilon}-s_{\varepsilon}\right|<\rho_{\varepsilon}^{q}<\sigma_{\varepsilon}^{q}<1.

This shows that
\left[\sum_{n=0}^{{\rm ni}(N)_{\varepsilon}}a_{n+M_{\varepsilon},\varepsilon}\right]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
,
because
s_{M}
,
s\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
, i.e.
(a_{n+M})_{n}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{s}
.
Similarly, proceeding as above, we can prove that
\left|\sum_{n=0}^{N}a_{n+M}-s-s_{M}\right|<\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}^{q}
.
∎

Once again, in some cases, it could happen that
\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-1}<M_{\varepsilon q}

for all
q\in\mathbb{N}
at least on some subpoint
L\subseteq_{0}I
. In
these cases, we have to consider the suitable gauge
\sigma
, e.g. taking

\sigma_{\varepsilon}:=\exp\left(-\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-1}}\right)\leq M_{\varepsilon q}^{-1}
.

We now study some basic properties of hyperfinite sums (
2.9
)
and hyperseries.

Lemma 17
.

Let
(a_{n})_{n}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{s}
,
and
M
,
N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
, then

\sum_{n=0}^{N+M}a_{n}-\sum_{n=0}^{N}a_{n}=\sum_{n=N+1}^{N+M}a_{n}.

(2.16)

Proof.

For simplicity, if
N=[N_{\varepsilon}]
,
M=[M_{\varepsilon}]
with
N_{\varepsilon}
,

M_{\varepsilon}\in\mathbb{N}
for all
\varepsilon
, then
\sum_{n=0}^{N}a_{n}=\left[\sum_{n=0}^{N_{\varepsilon}}a_{n\varepsilon}\right]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}

and

\sum_{n=0}^{N+M}a_{n}=\left[\sum_{n=0}^{N_{\varepsilon}+M_{\varepsilon}}a_{n\varepsilon}\right]=\left[\sum_{n=0}^{N_{\varepsilon}}a_{n\varepsilon}+\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}+1}^{N_{\varepsilon}+M_{\varepsilon}}a_{n\varepsilon}\right]=\sum_{n=0}^{N}a_{n}+\sum_{n=N+1}^{N+M}a_{n}.

∎

Lemma 18
.

If
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}
is convergent and

M\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
, then

\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-0.42224pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-0.42224pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-0.42224pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-0.42224pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\sum_{n=0}^{N+M}a_{n}.

Therefore, from (
2.16
), we also have

\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-0.42224pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-0.42224pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-0.42224pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-0.42224pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\sum_{n=N}^{N+M}a_{n}=0.

In particular,
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-0.42224pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-0.42224pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-0.42224pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-0.42224pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}=0
.

Proof.

Let
s=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}
. Directly from the definition of convergent
hyperseries (
2.10
), we have

\forall q\in\mathbb{N}\,\exists K\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,\forall N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}_{\geq K}:\left|\sum_{n=0}^{N}a_{n}-s\right|<\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}^{q}.

In particular, if
N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}_{\geq K}
, then also
N+M\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}_{\geq K}
,
which is our conclusion.
∎

Directly from Lem.
17
, we also have:

Corollary 19
.

Let
