\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}
be a convergent
hyperseries. Then adding a hyperfinite number of terms have no effect
on the convergence of the hyperseries, that is

\sum_{n=0}^{K}a_{n}+\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-0.42224pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-0.42224pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-0.42224pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-0.42224pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\sum_{n=K+1}^{N}a_{n}=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}\quad\forall K\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}.

Moreover, below we also have the usual connection between convergence
and absolute convergence of hyperseries:

Theorem 20
.

If the hyperseries
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}|a_{n}|

converges then
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}
converges. Moreover,

\left|\,\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}\right|\leq\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\left|a_{n}\right|.

Proof.

If we consider the hyperfinite sums
S_{N}=\sum_{n=0}^{N}a_{n}
and

T_{N}=\sum_{n=0}^{N}\left|a_{n}\right|
for all
N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
, then

(T_{N})_{N}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}
is a Cauchy hypersequence
by Thm.
10
. Then by the triangle inequality

\left|S_{N}-S_{M}\right|=\left|\sum_{n=N+1}^{M}a_{n}\right|\leq\sum_{n=N+1}^{M}|a_{n}|=|T_{M}-T_{N}|.

Thus,
(S_{N})_{N}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}
is also a Cauchy hypersequence,
so by Thm.
10
, it converges.
∎

In the classical direct comparison test, we need to assume a relation
of the form
a_{n}\leq b_{n}
for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
between general terms
of two real series. In case of hyperseries, we define a weaker but
natural relation in
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}{}_{\mathrm{s}}
:

Definition 21
.

Let
(a_{n})_{n}
,
(b_{n})_{n}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}{}_{\mathrm{s}}
,
then we say that
(a_{n})_{n}\leq(b_{n})_{n}
if

\forall N,M\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}:\sum_{n=N}^{M}a_{n}\leq\sum_{n=N}^{M}b_{n}\;\text{in}\;\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}.

(2.17)

Note explicitly, that if
M<_{L}N
on
L\subseteq_{0}I
, then
\sum_{n=N}^{M}a_{n}=_{L}\sum_{n=N}^{M}b_{n}=_{L}0
.
Therefore, (
2.17
) is equivalent to

\forall N,M\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}:M\geq N\implies\sum_{n=N}^{M}a_{n}\leq\sum_{n=N}^{M}b_{n}\;\text{in}\;\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}.

Note that the order relation in
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{\mathrm{u}}
is
[a_{n\varepsilon}]_{\mathrm{u}}\leq[b_{n\varepsilon}]_{\mathrm{u}}

if for some
[z_{n\varepsilon}]_{\mathrm{u}}=0
, we have

\forall^{0}\varepsilon\,\forall n\in\mathbb{N}:\ a_{n\varepsilon}\leq b_{n\varepsilon}+z_{n\varepsilon}.

We will implicitly use this relation in the ratio Thm.
23

and root Thm.
24
.

We have that
(\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}{}_{s},\leq)
is an ordered

\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}
-module, and if
(a_{n})_{n}\geq 0
, then
N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\mapsto\sum_{n=0}^{N}a_{n}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}

is increasing.

The direct comparison test for hyperseries can now be stated as follows:

Theorem 22
.

Let
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}

and
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}b_{n}
be hyperseries of generalized real numbers
with
(a_{n})_{n}
,
(b_{n})_{n}\geq 0
and such that

\exists N\in\mathbb{N}:(a_{n+N})_{n}\leq(b_{n+N})_{n}.

(2.18)

Then we have:

(i)

If
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}b_{n}
is convergent
then so is
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}
.

(ii)

If
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}
is divergent
to
+\infty
, then so is
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}b_{n}
.

Proof.

We first note that (
2.18
) can be simply written
as
(\alpha_{n})_{n}\leq(\beta_{n})_{n}
, where
\alpha_{n}:=a_{n+N}

and
\beta_{n}:=b_{n+N}
. Thereby, Corollary
19

implies that, without loss of generality, we can assume
N=0
. To
prove
(i)
, let us consider the partial
sum
A_{n}:=\sum_{i=0}^{n}a_{i}
and
B_{n}:=\sum_{i=0}^{n}b_{i}
,

n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
. Since
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}b_{n}
is convergent, we have

\exists\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}B_{n}=:B\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}
. The assumption
(a_{n})_{n}\leq(b_{n})_{n}

implies
A_{n}\leq B_{n}
. The hypersequences
(A_{n})_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}}
,

(B_{n})_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}}
are increasing because
(a_{n})_{n}
,
(b_{n})_{n}\geq 0

and hence
(B-B_{n})_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}}
decreases to zero because of the
convergence assumption. Now, for all
N
,
M\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
,
M\geq N
,
we have

A_{N}\leq A_{M}=\sum_{n=0}^{M}a_{n}=\sum_{n=0}^{N}a_{n}+\sum_{n=N+1}^{M}a_{n}\leq A_{N}+\sum_{n=N+1}^{M}b_{n}=A_{N}+(B-B_{N}).

(2.19)

Thereby, given
m
,
n\geq N
, applying (
2.19
) with

m
,
n
, we get that both
A_{n}
,
A_{m}
belongs to the interval

\left[A_{N},A_{N}+(B-B_{N})\right]
, whose length
B-B_{N}
decreases
to zero as
N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
goes to infinity. This shows that
(A_{n})_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}}

is a Cauchy hypersequence, and therefore
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}

converges.

The proof of
(ii)
follows directly from the
inequality
A_{n}\leq B_{n}
for each
n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
.
∎

Similar to the classical method, the geometric hyperseries leads to
a useful test for convergence of general hyperseries.

Theorem 23

(Ratio test)
.

Let
(a_{n})_{n}=[a_{n\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}

be such that
|a_{n}|>0
for all
n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
. Assume that for some

[k_{\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
, with
k_{\varepsilon}\in\mathbb{N}
, we have

\exists[L_{\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{>0}\>\forall^{0}\varepsilon\,\forall n\in\mathbb{N}_{\geq k_{\varepsilon}}:\;\frac{\left|a_{n+1,\varepsilon}\right|}{\left|a_{n\varepsilon}\right|}\leq L_{\varepsilon}.

(2.20)

Then, if
L<1
, the hyperseries
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}
converges
absolutely.

Proof.

Since
L<1
, by Example
15
(ii)
,
the hyperseries
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}L^{n}
is convergent. The ratio
assumption (
2.20
) yields the existence of
\left(z_{\varepsilon}\right)\sim_{\rho}0

such that for
\varepsilon
small (without loss of generality, we can assume
that all the following relations, starting from (
2.20
),
hold for all
\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}
), we have
\frac{\left|a_{k_{\varepsilon}+1,\varepsilon}\right|}{\left|a_{k_{\varepsilon},\varepsilon}\right|}\leq L_{\varepsilon}+z_{\varepsilon}

and hence
\left|a_{k_{\varepsilon}+1,\varepsilon}\right|\leq\left(L_{\varepsilon}+z_{\varepsilon}\right)\left|a_{k_{\varepsilon},\varepsilon}\right|

and recursively, we have
\left|a_{k_{\varepsilon}+N+1,\varepsilon}\right|\leq\left(L_{\varepsilon}+z_{\varepsilon}\right)\left|a_{k_{\varepsilon}+N,\varepsilon}\right|\leq\ldots\leq\left(L_{\varepsilon}+z_{\varepsilon}\right)^{N+1}\left|a_{k_{\varepsilon},\varepsilon}\right|

for
\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}
. Now the series

\sum_{n=k_{\varepsilon}+1}^{\infty}\left|a_{n\varepsilon}\right|\leq\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\left(L_{\varepsilon}+z_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n}\left|a_{k_{\varepsilon},\varepsilon}\right|.

Since
\left(z_{\varepsilon}\right)\sim_{\rho}0
and
L<1
then
L=\left[L_{\varepsilon}+z_{\varepsilon}\right]<1
,
and hence for all
N=[N_{\varepsilon}]
,
M=[M_{\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
with
N_{\varepsilon}\leq M_{\varepsilon}

\sum_{n=\max(N,k)}^{M}\left|a_{n}\right|\leq\left|a_{k}\right|\sum_{n=\max(N,k)}^{M}\left[L_{\varepsilon}+z_{\varepsilon}\right]^{n}=\left|a_{k}\right|\sum_{n=\max(N,k)}^{M}L^{n}.

By direct comparison test for hyperseries of generalized real numbers
(Thm.
22
), we can conclude that

\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\left|a_{n}\right|
is convergent (and by Thm.
20
,
the hyperseries
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}
is also convergent).
∎

Proceeding as in the previous proof, i.e. by generalizing the classical
proof for series of complex numbers, we also have the following root
test.

Theorem 24

(Root test)
.

Let
(a_{n})_{n}=[a_{n\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}
.
Assume that for some
[k_{\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
, with
k_{\varepsilon}\in\mathbb{N}
,
we have

\exists[L_{\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{>0}\,\forall^{0}\varepsilon\,\forall n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}_{\geq k_{\varepsilon}}:\;\left|a_{n\varepsilon}\right|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}}}\leq L_{\varepsilon}.

Then, if
L<1
, the hyperseries
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}
converges
absolutely.

Proof.

We can proceed as in the previous theorem considering that if
\left|a_{n}\right|\leq L^{n}
,
then
\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}_{\geq k}}\left|a_{n}\right|\leq\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}_{\geq k}}L^{n}
.
∎

2.3.
Definition and properties of hyper-power series

Classically, a power series is “a series of the form
\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}a_{n}(z-c)^{n}
”
(here, it is clearly meant that
a_{n}
,
z
,
c\in\mathbb{C}
). If we
do not consider its convergence, we talk of
formal power series

(then
z
is some kind of
indeterminate
, not a numerical variable).
Otherwise, we need to take
z
in a suitable
set of convergence
,
and hence the notion of
radius of convergence
is a natural
preliminary step.

In the ring
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
, we proceed similarly, but necessarily
being somehow more formal. Firstly, we always have to recall that
our definitions must be independent from representatives. Secondly,
in our setting we can have that the radius of converge
r=+\infty
,
but the values of the hyperseries must be anyway given by moderate
nets. This implies that a given hyperseries (e.g. the exponential
one) cannot have
B_{r}(0)=\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
as
set of convergence
.

Definition 25
.

Let
z
,
c\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
. We say
(b_{n})_{n}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}\left\llbracket z-c\right\rrbracket

if and only if there exist
(a_{n\varepsilon})_{n,\varepsilon}\in\mathbb{R}^{\mathbb{N}\times I}

and representatives
[z_{\varepsilon}]=z
,
[c_{\varepsilon}]=c
such that

(b_{n})_{n}=[a_{n\varepsilon}\cdot(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}]_{{\rm s}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}.

(2.21)

Elements of
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}\left\llbracket z-c\right\rrbracket

are called
formal hyperpower series (formal HPS)
because
we are not considering their convergence. In other words, a formal
HPS is a hyperseries (i.e. an equivalence class
(b_{n})_{n}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}

in the space of sequences of hyperseries, see Def.
12
,
where we can always consider hyperfinite sums) of the form
\left[a_{n\varepsilon}\cdot(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right]_{{\rm s}}
.

Remark 26
.

(i)

We explicitly note that
z-c
is not an indeterminate, like in the
case of formal power series
\mathbb{C}[z]
, but a generalized number of

\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
. For example, if
z-c=y-d
then
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}\left\llbracket z-c\right\rrbracket=\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}\left\llbracket y-d\right\rrbracket
.

(ii)

In Example
15
(iv)
, we already
proved that if
z
satisfies
|z|\leq-K\log\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}
, for some

K\in\mathbb{R}
, then
\left(\frac{z^{n}}{n!}\right)_{n}=\left[\frac{z_{\varepsilon}^{n}}{n!}\right]_{{\rm s}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}\left\llbracket z\right\rrbracket

is a formal HPS. This clearly shows that we can consider any hyperfinite
sum
\sum_{n=N}^{M}\frac{z^{n}}{n!}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
,
N
,
M\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
,
only if
z\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
is of logarithmic type, so that we can
apply Def.
12
of hyperseries.

(iii)

Actually, the term
[a_{n\varepsilon}\cdot(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}]_{{\rm s}}

in (
2.21
) does not depend on
n\in\mathbb{N}
. Similarly,
the notation
(b_{n})_{n}
does not depend on
n
and has only the
main aim to recall the sequence of coefficients of the formal HPS.
We have a formally similar situation in the notation
\int f(x)\,\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}x}{\hbox{\rm d}x}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}x$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}x$}}

that does not depend on
x
.

As we mentioned above, the previous Def.
25
sets
immediate problems concerning independence of representatives. Taking
different representatives
[\overline{a}_{n\varepsilon}]=[a_{n\varepsilon}]
,
[\overline{z}_{\varepsilon}]=z
,

[\overline{c}_{\varepsilon}]=c
, do we have
[a_{n\varepsilon}\cdot(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}]_{{\rm s}}=[\overline{a}_{n\varepsilon}\cdot(\overline{z}_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}]_{{\rm s}}
?
Let
a_{n}=[a_{n\varepsilon}]
be such that
a_{n\varepsilon}:=0
if
\varepsilon<\frac{1}{n+1}

and
a_{n\varepsilon}:=1
otherwise, then
[a_{n\varepsilon}]=0
but the corresponding
series is not zero:

\forall N=[N_{\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}:\sum_{n=0}^{N_{\varepsilon}}a_{n\varepsilon}=\sum_{n=\left\lceil\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right\rceil-1}^{N_{\varepsilon}}1=N_{\varepsilon}-\left\lceil\frac{1}{\varepsilon}\right\rceil+2.

This means that
(b_{n})_{n}:=\left[a_{n\varepsilon}(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right]_{{\rm s}}

and
(\bar{b}_{n})_{n}:=\left[\bar{a}_{n\varepsilon}(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right]_{{\rm s}}
,
for
z=1
,
c=0
, yield two different formal HPS and hence, in general
the operation

((a_{n\varepsilon})_{n,\varepsilon},(z_{\varepsilon}),(c_{\varepsilon}))\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}^{\mathbb{N}}\times\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}^{2}\mapsto(b_{n})_{n}:=\left[a_{n\varepsilon}(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right]_{s}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{\mathrm{s}}

is not well-defined. The following notion of negligibility (see Def.
27

below) naturally emerges in proving this independence of representative
and in showing that the following definition of radius of convergence
is well-defined (see Lem.
28
).

The idea to define the radius of convergence corresponding to coefficients

(a_{n\varepsilon})_{n,\varepsilon}\in\mathbb{R}^{\mathbb{N}\times I}
is that it does not matter
if

\left(\limsup_{n\rightarrow+\infty}|a_{n\varepsilon}|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}}}\right)^{-1}\in\mathbb{R}\cup\{+\infty\}

yields a non
\rho
-moderate net (for example for
\varepsilon\in L\subseteq_{0}I
)
because this case would intuitively identify a radius of convergence
larger than any number in
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}
:

Definition 27
.

(i)

Let
\bar{\mathbb{R}}:=\mathbb{R}\cup\{-\infty,+\infty\}
be the extended real number
system with the usual (partially defined) operations. We set
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\overline{\mathbb{R}}:=\bar{\mathbb{R}}^{I}/\sim_{\rho}
,
where for
arbitrary

(x_{\varepsilon})
,
(y_{\varepsilon})\in\bar{\mathbb{R}}^{I}
,
as usual, we define

(x_{\varepsilon})\sim_{\rho}(y_{\varepsilon})\quad:\Longleftrightarrow\quad\forall q\in\mathbb{N}\,\forall^{0}\varepsilon:\ |x_{\varepsilon}-y_{\varepsilon}|\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{q}.

In
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\overline{\mathbb{R}}
, we can also consider the standard order
relation

x\leq y\quad:\Longleftrightarrow\quad\exists[x_{\varepsilon}]=x,\>[y_{\varepsilon}]=y\>\forall^{0}\varepsilon:\ x_{\varepsilon}\leq y_{\varepsilon}.

Note that
\left(\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\overline{\mathbb{R}}\setminus\{-\infty\},+,\leq\right)

is an ordered group but, since we are considering arbitrary nets
\bar{\mathbb{R}}^{I}
,
the set
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\overline{\mathbb{R}}
is not a ring: e.g.
+\infty\cdot 0

is still undefined and
+\infty\cdot[z_{\varepsilon}]=[+\infty]
for all

(z_{\varepsilon})\in\mathbb{R}_{>0}^{I}
.

(ii)

Moreover, we denote by
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm c}}:=\left(\mathbb{C}^{\mathbb{N}\times I}\right)_{\rho}/\simeq_{\rho}

the quotient ring of
coefficients for HPS
, where

(a_{n\varepsilon})_{n,\varepsilon}\in\left(\mathbb{C}^{\mathbb{N}\times I}\right)_{\rho}\quad:\Longleftrightarrow\quad\exists Q,R\in\mathbb{N}\,\forall^{0}\varepsilon\,\forall n\in\mathbb{N}:\ |a_{n\varepsilon}|\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-nQ-R}

(2.22)

is the ring of
weakly
\rho
-moderate
nets, and

(a_{n\varepsilon})_{n,\varepsilon}\simeq_{\rho}(\bar{a}_{n,\varepsilon})_{n,\varepsilon}\quad:\Longleftrightarrow\quad\forall q,r\in\mathbb{N}\,\forall^{0}\varepsilon\,\forall n\in\mathbb{N}:\ |a_{n\varepsilon}-\bar{a}_{n\varepsilon}|\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{nq+r},

(2.23)

in this case, we say that these two nets are
strongly
\rho
-equivalent
.
Equivalence classes of
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm c}}
are denoted by
(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}:=[a_{n\varepsilon}]_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm c}}
.

(iii)

Finally, if
(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}=[a_{n\varepsilon}]_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm c}}
,
then we set
\text{rad}(a_{n})_{n\varepsilon}^{{\rm c}}:=r_{\varepsilon}
and
\text{rad}(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}=:[r_{\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\overline{\mathbb{R}}
,
where

r_{\varepsilon}:=\left(\limsup_{n\rightarrow+\infty}|a_{n\varepsilon}|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}}}\right)^{-1}\in\mathbb{R}\cup\{+\infty\}.

(2.24)

In the following lemma, we prove that
\text{rad}(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}

is well-defined:

Lemma 28
.

Let
(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}=[a_{n\varepsilon}]_{{\rm c}}=[\bar{a}_{n\varepsilon}]_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm c}}
.
Define
r_{\varepsilon}
as in (
2.24
) and similarly define
\bar{r}_{\varepsilon}

using
\bar{a}_{n\varepsilon}
. Then
(r_{\varepsilon})\sim_{\rho}(\bar{r}_{\varepsilon})
,
and hence
[r_{\varepsilon}]=[\bar{r}_{\varepsilon}]
in
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\overline{\mathbb{R}}
.

Proof.

For all
\varepsilon\in I
and all
n\in\mathbb{N}_{>0}
, we have
|\bar{a}_{n\varepsilon}|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}}}\leq\left(\left|\bar{a}_{n\varepsilon}-a_{n\varepsilon}\right|+|a_{n\varepsilon}|\right)^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}}}
.
The binomial formula yields
(x+y)\leq\left(x^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}}}+y^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}}}\right)^{n}

for all
x
,
y\in\mathbb{R}_{\geq 0}
, so that
|\bar{a}_{n\varepsilon}|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}}}\leq\left|\bar{a}_{n\varepsilon}-a_{n\varepsilon}\right|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}}}+|a_{n\varepsilon}|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}}}
.
Setting
r=0
in (
2.23
), for all
q\in\mathbb{N}
and for

\varepsilon
small we have

\forall n\in\mathbb{N}:\left|a_{n\varepsilon}-\bar{a}_{n\varepsilon}\right|\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{nq}.

Therefore, for the same
\varepsilon
we get
|\bar{a}_{n\varepsilon}|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}}}\leq q_{\varepsilon}^{q}+|a_{n\varepsilon}|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}}}
.
Taking the limit superior, we obtain
\limsup_{n\rightarrow+\infty}|\bar{a}_{n\varepsilon}|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}}}\leq q_{\varepsilon}^{q}+\limsup_{n\rightarrow+\infty}|a_{n\varepsilon}|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}}}
.
Inverting the role of
(a_{n\varepsilon})_{n,\varepsilon}
and
(\bar{a}_{n\varepsilon})_{n,\varepsilon}

we finally obtain

\forall^{0}\varepsilon:-\rho_{\varepsilon}^{q}\leq\limsup_{n\rightarrow+\infty}|a_{n\varepsilon}|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}}}-\limsup_{n\rightarrow+\infty}|\bar{a}_{n\varepsilon}|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}}}\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{q},

which proves the claim.
∎

Remark 29
.

(i)

If
(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}=[a_{n\varepsilon}]_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm c}}
,
then for each fixed
n\in\mathbb{N}
, we have that
[(a_{n\varepsilon})_{\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
,
i.e. the net
(a_{n\varepsilon})_{\varepsilon}
is
\rho
-moderate. This is the
main motivation to consider the exponent “
-R
” in (
2.22
)
(recall that in our notation
0\in\mathbb{N}
): without the term “
-R
”,
the only possibility to have
(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm c}}

is that
|a_{0}|\leq 1
, which is an unnecessary limitation. Similarly,
we can motivate why we are considering the quantifier “
\forall n\in\mathbb{N}
”
in the same formula (instead of, e.g., “
\exists N\in\mathbb{N}\>\forall n\in\mathbb{N}_{\geq N}
”).
Moreover, Thm.
33
will motivate
why in (
2.22
) we consider the uniform property
“
\forall^{0}\varepsilon\,\forall n\in\mathbb{N}
” and not “
\forall n\in\mathbb{N}\>\forall^{0}\varepsilon
”.

(ii)

Condition (
2.22
) of being weakly
\rho
-moderate
represents a constrain on what coefficients
a_{n}
we can consider
in a hyperseries. For example, if
(a_{n})_{n\in\mathbb{N}}
is a sequence
of complex numbers satisfying
|a_{n}|<p(n)
, where
p\in\mathbb{R}[x]

is a polynomial, then
p(n)\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-nQ}
for all
\varepsilon

sufficiently small and for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
if
Q\geq\max\left(1,\max\left\{-\frac{\log n}{p(n)\log\rho_{\varepsilon}}:n<N_{1}\right\}\right)
,
where
\frac{\log n}{p(n)}\leq 1
for all
n\geq N_{1}
and
-\frac{1}{\log\rho_{\varepsilon}}\leq 1
.
Hence
(a_{n})_{n,\varepsilon}\in\left(\mathbb{R}^{\mathbb{N}\times I}\right)_{\rho}
is
weakly
\rho
