-moderate. On the contrary, we cannot have
n^{n}\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-nQ-R}=\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-R}\left(\frac{1}{\rho_{\varepsilon}^{Q}}\right)^{n}

for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
. Similarly
(n!)_{n\in\mathbb{N}}
is not weakly
\rho
-moderate
and hence our theory does not apply to a “hyperseries” of the
form
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}n!z^{n}
.

(iii)

Let
a_{n\varepsilon}=\rho_{\varepsilon}^{\frac{n+1}{\varepsilon}}
, so that
[a_{n\varepsilon}]_{{\rm c}}=0
.
The corresponding radius of convergence is
r_{\varepsilon}=\lim_{n\rightarrow+\infty}|a_{n\varepsilon}|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}}}=\rho_{\varepsilon}^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{\varepsilon}}}

which is not
\rho
-moderate. In general, if
\text{rad}(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}=[r_{\varepsilon}]=:r\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\overline{\mathbb{R}}
,
we can have different behavior on different subpoints, e.g.
r|_{L_{1}}=+\infty
,

r|_{L_{2}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}
,
r|_{L_{3}}
is non
\rho
-moderate,
etc., where
L_{i}\subseteq_{0}I
. This behavior is studied in Thm.

28
below.

(iv)

Let
(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}=[a_{n\varepsilon}]_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm c}}

and assume that for all
\varepsilon
there exists
r_{\varepsilon}:=\left(\lim_{n\rightarrow+\infty}|a_{n\varepsilon}|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}}}\right)^{-1}

such that
r:=[r_{\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}
. Then, we have
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}|a_{n}|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}}}=\frac{1}{r}

and
r=\text{rad}(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}
.

The following lemma represents a useful tool to deal with radius of
convergence. It essentially states that the radius of convergence
cannot be zero (but it can be an invertible infinitesimal), it is
finite or it equals
+\infty
at least on some subpoint, and behaves
like a supremum (see also
[
7
]
).

Theorem 30
.

Let
(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm c}}

and
r=[r_{\varepsilon}]=\emph{rad}(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\overline{\mathbb{R}}
,
then we have

(i)

r>0
;

(ii)

r<+\infty
or
r=_{\text{\rm s}}+\infty
;

(iii)

If
q\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}
and
q<r
, then
\exists s\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{>0}:q<s\leq r
.

Proof.

(i)
: Assume that
|a_{n\varepsilon}|\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-nQ-R}

for all
\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}
and for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
. Then
\limsup_{n\rightarrow+\infty}|a_{n\varepsilon}|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}}}\leq\lim_{n\rightarrow+\infty}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-Q-\frac{R}{n}}=\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-Q}
,
i.e.
r_{\varepsilon}\geq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{Q}
.

(ii)
: Set
L:=\left\{\varepsilon\in I:r_{\varepsilon}=+\infty\right\}
.
If
L\subseteq_{0}I
, then
r=_{L}+\infty
. Otherwise
(0,\varepsilon_{0}]\cap L=\emptyset

for some
\varepsilon_{0}
, i.e.
r_{\varepsilon}<+\infty
for all
\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}
.

(iii)
: Assume that
r>q
. Take representative

[r_{\varepsilon}]=r
and
[q_{\varepsilon}]=q
. By
[
9
, Lem. 3]
, we
can find
m\in\mathbb{N}
such that
r_{\varepsilon}>q_{\varepsilon}+\rho_{\varepsilon}^{m}

for small
\varepsilon
. For all
\varepsilon
, take
s_{\varepsilon}:=\min\left(r_{\varepsilon},q_{\varepsilon}+2\rho_{\varepsilon}^{m}\right)
.
Then
s:[s_{\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{>0}
and
q<s\leq r
.
∎

Even if the radius of convergence of the exponential hyperseries is

\text{rad}\left(\frac{1}{n!}\right)_{\mathrm{c}}=+\infty
, we have
that
e^{z}=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\frac{z^{n}}{n!}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
implies

|z|\leq\log\left(\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}^{-R}\right)
for some
R\in\mathbb{N}
: in other
words, the constraint to get a
\rho
-moderate number implies that
even if
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\frac{z^{n}}{n!}
converges at
z
, the
exponential HPS does
not
converge in the entire
B\left(z\right)_{r}=\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
,
where
r:=\text{rad}\left(\frac{1}{n!}\right)_{\mathrm{c}}=+\infty
.

In several steps below, we will also see that it is also very useful
to have that the generalized number
\left[\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}a_{n\varepsilon}(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
,
where
N_{\varepsilon}\in\mathbb{N}
and
M_{\varepsilon}\in\mathbb{N}\cup\{+\infty\}
are
\rho
-moderate
nets, is well-defined as a function of
z=[z_{\varepsilon}]
.

Finally, in all our examples, if the HPS
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}(z-c)^{n}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}

converges, then it converges exactly to
\left[\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}a_{n\varepsilon}(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
.
These observations motivate the following definition of
set
of convergence
:

Definition 31
.

Let
(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm c}}

and
c\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
. The set of convergence

\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left((a_{n})_{{\rm c}},c\right)

is the set of all
z\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
satisfying

(i)

|z-c|<\text{rad}(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}
,

and such that there exist representatives
[z_{\varepsilon}]=z
,
[a_{n\varepsilon}]_{{\rm c}}=(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}
,
and
[c_{\varepsilon}]=c
satisfying the following conditions:

(ii)

\left[a_{n\varepsilon}(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right]_{{\rm s}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}\left\llbracket z-c\right\rrbracket
,
i.e. we have a formal HPS;

(iii)

\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}(z-c)^{n}=\left[\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}a_{n\varepsilon}(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
;

(iv)

For all representatives
[\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}]=z
,
the derivative net is
\rho
-moderate:

\left(\frac{\text{d}}{\text{d}z}\left(\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}a_{n\varepsilon}(z-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right)_{z=\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}}\right)\in\mathbb{C}_{\rho}.

(2.25)

Note that condition
(ii)
is necessary because in
(iii)

we use a HPS; on the other hand, condition
(iii)
and
(iv)

state that we have a GHF defined by the net of holomorphic functions

\left(\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}a_{n\varepsilon}(z-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right)
. As
for GHF, see
[
9
, Thm. 26]
, condition
(iv)

only on first derivative suffices to prove that we actually have a
GHF in
z
.

We immediately note that
z\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left((a_{n})_{{\rm c}},c\right)

if and only if
z-c\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left((a_{n})_{{\rm c}},0\right)
,
and because of this property without loss of generality we will frequently
assume
c=0
. We also note that condition
(iii)
states
that the HPS
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}z^{n}
converges, and it does
exactly to the generalized number generated by
\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}a_{n\varepsilon}(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}
.

As expected, for HPS the set of convergence is never trivial:

Theorem 32
.

Let
(a_{n})_{c}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm c}}

and
c\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
. Then

\exists q\in\mathbb{N}:B_{\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}^{q}}(c)\subseteq\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left((a_{n})_{{\rm c}},c\right).

Proof.

From Thm.
30
(i)
, we have

r:=\text{rad}(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}\geq\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}^{q_{1}}
for some
q_{1}\in\mathbb{N}
.
We also have
|a_{n\varepsilon}|\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-nQ-R}
from
2.22
.
We have to find
q\in\mathbb{N}_{\geq q_{1}}
so that
B_{\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}^{q}}(c)\subseteq\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left((a_{n})_{{\rm c}},c\right)
.
To prove property Def.
31
(ii)
,
for
N_{\varepsilon}
,
M_{\varepsilon}\in\mathbb{N}
and for
\varepsilon
small, we estimate

\left|\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}a_{n\varepsilon}(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right|\leq\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-nQ-R}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{nq}=\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-R}\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-nQ+nq}.

Therefore, taking
q:=\max\left(1+Q,q_{1}\right)
, we get

\left|\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}a_{n\varepsilon}(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right|\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-R}\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{n}\leq\frac{\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-R}}{1-\rho_{\varepsilon}},

and this proves Def.
31
(ii)
.
Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{n=0}^{M_{\varepsilon}}a_{n\varepsilon}(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}-\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}a_{n\varepsilon}(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{n=M_{\varepsilon}+1}^{+\infty}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{n}\leq\frac{\rho_{\varepsilon}^{M_{\varepsilon}+1}}{1-\rho_{\varepsilon}}.

Since
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}^{M+1}=0
, this proves Def.
31
(iii)
.
Finally, for all representatives
[\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}]=z
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{\text{d}}{\text{d}z}\left(\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}a_{n\varepsilon}(z-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right)_{z=\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}na_{n\varepsilon}(\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n-1}

and hence

\displaystyle\left|\frac{\text{d}}{\text{d}z}\left(\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}a_{n\varepsilon}(z-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right)_{z=\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}n\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-nQ-R}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{\left(n-1\right)q}

\displaystyle=\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-R-q}\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}n\rho_{\varepsilon}^{(q-Q)n}

\displaystyle=\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-R-q}\frac{\rho_{\varepsilon}^{q-Q}}{(1-\rho_{\varepsilon}^{q-Q})^{2}}.

In the last step, we use
q\geq Q+1
and the series
\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}ny^{n}=\frac{y}{(1-y)^{2}}

for
|y|<1
. This proves property
(iv)
.
∎

We can now prove independence from representatives both in Def.
31

and in Def.
25
:

Theorem 33
.

Let
(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}=[a_{n\varepsilon}]_{{\rm c}}=[\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}]_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm c}}
,

z=[z_{\varepsilon}]=[\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}]
,
c=[c_{\varepsilon}]=[\hat{c}_{\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
.
Assume that
z\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left((a_{n})_{{\rm c}},c\right)
.
Then,

(i)

The nets
(a_{n\varepsilon})_{n,\varepsilon}
,
(z_{\varepsilon})
,
and
(c_{\varepsilon})
also satisfy all the conditions of Def.
31

of set of convergence.

(ii)

\left[a_{n\varepsilon}(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right]_{{\rm s}}=\left[\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}(\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}-\hat{c}_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right]_{{\rm s}}
,
where the equality is in
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}
(see Def.
12
).

Proof.

(i)
: Since we have similar steps for several
claims, let
N_{\varepsilon}\in\mathbb{N}
and
M_{\varepsilon}\in\mathbb{N}\cup\{+\infty\}
,
so that a term of the form
\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}b_{n\varepsilon}

represents both the ordinary series
\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}b_{n\varepsilon}

or the finite sum
\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}b_{n\varepsilon}
. From Def.
31

of set of convergence, we get the existence of representatives
[\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}]=z\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
,

[\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}]_{{\rm c}}=(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}
and
[\hat{c}_{\varepsilon}]=c

satisfying Def.
31
. Set
y_{\varepsilon}:=z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon}
,

y:=[y_{\varepsilon}]
,
\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}:=\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}-\hat{c}_{\varepsilon}

and
\hat{y}:=[\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}]
. Let
r:=[r_{\varepsilon}]=\text{rad}(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}

be the radius of convergence. Take
s\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}
satisfying
|\hat{y}|<s\leq r

and a representative
[s_{\varepsilon}]=s
such that
|\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}|<s_{\varepsilon}\leq r_{\varepsilon}

for all
\varepsilon
small. Set
b_{n\varepsilon}:=a_{n\varepsilon}-\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}

and
h_{\varepsilon}:=y_{\varepsilon}-\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}
. We have

\displaystyle\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}a_{n\varepsilon}y_{\varepsilon}^{n}

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}\left(\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}+b_{n\varepsilon}\right)\left(\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}+h_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n}

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}\left(\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}+h_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n}+\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}b_{n\varepsilon}\left(\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}+h_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n}

(2.26)

and

\displaystyle\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}na_{n\varepsilon}y_{\varepsilon}^{n-1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}n\left(\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}+b_{n\varepsilon}\right)\left(\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}+h_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n-1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}n\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}\left(\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}+h_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n-1}+\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}nb_{n\varepsilon}\left(\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}+h_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n-1}.

(2.27)

Since
h:=[h_{\varepsilon}]=0
, we also have
|\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}|+|h_{\varepsilon}|<s_{\varepsilon}\leq r_{\varepsilon}

for all
\varepsilon
small. For the same
\varepsilon
, assume that
|b_{n\varepsilon}|\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{np+q}

for fixed arbitrary
p
,
q\in\mathbb{N}
. We first consider the second
summand of (
2.26
) and (
2.27
):

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}b_{n\varepsilon}\left(\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}+h_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{np+q}s_{\varepsilon}^{n}\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{q}\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}\left(\rho_{\varepsilon}^{p}s_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n}\text{ and}

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}nb_{n\varepsilon}\left(\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}+h_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n-1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}n\rho_{\varepsilon}^{np+q}s_{\varepsilon}^{n-1}\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{q}\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}n\left(\rho_{\varepsilon}^{p}s_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n-1}.

Since
s\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}
, we can take
p\in\mathbb{N}
sufficiently large
so that
\rho_{\varepsilon}^{p}s_{\varepsilon}<1
. This implies

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}b_{n\varepsilon}\left(\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}+h_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\rho_{\varepsilon}^{q}}{\left(1-\rho_{\varepsilon}^{p}s_{\varepsilon}\right)}

\displaystyle\text{and }\left|\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}nb_{n\varepsilon}\left(\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}+h_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n-1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\rho_{\varepsilon}^{q}}{\left(1-\rho_{\varepsilon}^{p}s_{\varepsilon}\right)^{2}}.

Thereby, for
q\rightarrow+\infty
, this summand defines a negligible
net. For the first summand of (
2.27
), we
have

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}\left(\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}+h_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n}-\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}^{n}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}\left(\sum_{k=0}^{n}\binom{n-1}{k}\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}^{n-k}h_{\varepsilon}^{k}\right)-\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}^{n}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n-1}\binom{n-1}{k}\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}^{n-k}h_{\varepsilon}^{k}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=\left|h_{\varepsilon}\right|\left|\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n-1}\binom{n-1}{k}\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}^{n-k}h_{\varepsilon}^{k-1}\right)\right|

(2.28)

and

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}n\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}\left(\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}+h_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n-1}-\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}n\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}^{n-1}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}n\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}\left(\sum_{k=0}^{n-1}\binom{n-1}{k}\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}^{(n-1)-k}h_{\varepsilon}^{k}\right)-\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}n\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}^{n-1}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}n\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n-1}\binom{n-1}{k}\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}^{(n-1)-k}h_{\varepsilon}^{k}\right)\right|

\displaystyle=\left|h_{\varepsilon}\right|\left|\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}n\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{n-1}\binom{n-1}{k}\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}^{(n-1)-k}h_{\varepsilon}^{k-1}\right)\right|.

(2.29)

Thereby, the right hand side of (
2.28
)
and (
2.29
) is negligible because
h:=[h_{\varepsilon}]=0
.
We can hence state that

\left(\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}a_{n\varepsilon}y_{\varepsilon}^{n}\right)\sim_{\rho}\left(\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}\left(\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}+h_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n}\right)\text{ and }

(2.30)

\left(\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}na_{n\varepsilon}y_{\varepsilon}^{n-1}\right)\sim_{\rho}\left(\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}n\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}\left(\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}+h_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n-1}\right).

These prove that
[a_{n\varepsilon}\cdot y_{\varepsilon}^{n}]_{s}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}
,
the moderateness of
\left(\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}a_{n\varepsilon}y_{\varepsilon}^{n}\right)
,
and Def.
31
(iv)
. Hence,
we also proved claim
(ii)
. Moreover, from
(
2.30
), we have
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}[\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}]\cdot[\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}]^{n}

converges to
\left[\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}\hat{a}_{n\varepsilon}\hat{y}_{\varepsilon}^{n}\right]=\left[\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}a_{n\varepsilon}y_{\varepsilon}^{n}\right]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
.
∎

In the following examples, we start studying geometric hyperseries,
which are convergent HPS:

Example 34

(Geometric HPS)
.

Assume that
z\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
and
|z|<1
.
We have

\left[\left|\sum_{n=0}^{N_{\varepsilon}}z_{\varepsilon}^{n}\right|\right]\leq\left[\left|\frac{1-\left|z_{\varepsilon}^{N_{\varepsilon}+1}\right|}{1-z_{\varepsilon}}\right|\right]\leq\frac{2}{1-z}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}.

This shows that
(z^{n})_{n}=[z_{\varepsilon}^{n}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}
.
Moreover, from Ex.
15
(ii)
,
we have

\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}z^{n}=\frac{1}{1-z}.

Since coefficients
a_{n\varepsilon}=1
, we have
[a_{n\varepsilon}]_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm c}}
,

\text{rad}(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}=1
, and
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left((1)_{{\rm c}},0\right)\subseteq B_{1}(0)
.
Now, take
z=[z_{\varepsilon}]\in B_{1}(0)
, with
|z_{\varepsilon}|<1
for all

\varepsilon
, we have

\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}z^{n}=\left[\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}z_{\varepsilon}^{n}\right]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}

and if
[\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}]=z
is another representative and
k\in\mathbb{N}_{>0}
,
then
\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}\in B^{{\scriptscriptstyle\text{\rm E}}}_{1}(0)
and we get
\left(\sum_{n=k}^{+\infty}n\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}^{n-1}\right)=\left(\frac{1}{\left(1-\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}\right)^{2}}\right)\in\mathbb{C}_{\rho}

because
|1-z|>0
is invertible. In particular, the convergence of
the geometric hyperseries
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}^{n}=\frac{1}{1-\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}}
.

Example 35

(Exponential)
.

We clearly have
\left(\frac{1}{n!}\right)_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm c}}

and
\text{rad}\left(\frac{1}{n!}\right)_{{\rm c}}=+\infty
, i.e.
we have coefficients for an HPS with infinite radius of convergence.
Set

C:=\left\{z\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}\mid\exists K\in\mathbb{N}:|z|<-K\log\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}\right\}.

For all
z=[z_{\varepsilon}]\in C
and all
N_{\varepsilon}
,
M_{\varepsilon}\in\mathbb{N}
,
we have
\left|\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}\frac{z_{\varepsilon}^{n}}{n!}\right|\leq e^{|z_{\varepsilon}|}\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-K}
for

\varepsilon
small, and this shows that
\left(\frac{z^{n}}{n!}\right)_{n}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}\left\llbracket z\right\rrbracket
,
i.e. for all
z\in C
, we have a formal HPS. We finally want to prove
that
C=\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(\frac{1}{n!}\right)_{{\rm c}},0\right)
.
The inclusion
\supseteq
follows directly from Def.
31
.
If
z=[z_{\varepsilon}]\in C
, then
\left(\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}\frac{z_{\varepsilon}^{n}}{n!}\right)

and
\left(\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}n\frac{z_{\varepsilon}^{n-1}}{n!}\right)\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
.
To prove Def.
31
(iii)
, assume
that
|z_{\varepsilon}|<-K\log\rho_{\varepsilon}=:M_{\varepsilon}
for all
\varepsilon
and
set
M:=[M_{\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}
. Take
N=\left[N_{\varepsilon}\right]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}

such that
\frac{M}{N+1}<\frac{1}{2}
, so that
\frac{M^{n+1}}{(n+1)!}<\frac{1}{2^{n+1}}

and hence
\left|\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}+1}^{+\infty}\frac{z_{\varepsilon}^{n}}{n!}\right|\leq\sum_{n\geq N_{\varepsilon}}\frac{1}{2^{n}}\rightarrow 0

as
N\rightarrow+\infty
,
N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
. Similarly, we can consider
trigonometric functions whose set of convergence is the whole of
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
.

Example 36

(Dirac delta)
.

Recall from
[
9
, Sec. 3]
that we
can define a Dirac delta embedded as a GHF, by
\delta_{\varepsilon}(z):=\left(\delta\ast\mu_{\varepsilon}\right)(z)=\mu_{\varepsilon}(z)=\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-2}\mu\left(\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-1}z\right)

where
\mu\in\mathcal{C}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})
is an entire function
such that
\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\mu(x)\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}x}{\hbox{\rm d}x}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}x$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}x$}}=1
(
\mu
is called entire
mollifier in
[
9
]
). We have that
\left(\frac{\delta^{(n)}(0)}{n!}\right)_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm c}}

and
\text{rad}\left(\frac{\delta^{(n)}(0)}{n!}\right)_{{\rm c}}=+\infty
.
In fact, for
n\in\mathbb{N}
, we have
\left|\frac{\delta_{\varepsilon}^{(n)}(0)}{n!}\right|=\left|\frac{\mu^{(n)}(0)}{n!}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-2n-2}\right|\leq\frac{1}{n!}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-2n-2}\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-2n-2}

and

r_{\varepsilon}^{-1}=\limsup_{n\rightarrow+\infty}\left|\frac{\delta_{\varepsilon}^{(n)}(0)}{n!}\right|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}}}=\limsup_{n\rightarrow+\infty}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-2(1+\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}})}\left|\frac{\mu^{(n)}(0)}{n!}\right|^{\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}}}=\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-2(1+\nicefrac{{1}}{{n}})}\cdot 0=0.

For all
z=[z_{\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
and all
N_{\varepsilon}
,
M_{\varepsilon}\in\mathbb{N}
,
we have

\left|\sum_{n=N_{\varepsilon}}^{M_{\varepsilon}}\frac{\delta_{\varepsilon}^{(n)}(0)}{n!}z_{\varepsilon}^{n}\right|\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-2}\cdot\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}\frac{|\mu^{(n)}(0)|}{n!}\left|\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-1}z_{\varepsilon}\right|^{n}=\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-2}\mu\left(|\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-1}z_{\varepsilon}|\right)\in\mathbb{C}_{\rho}.

This proves that
\left(\frac{\delta^{(n)}(0)}{n!}\right)_{n}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}\left\llbracket z\right\rrbracket
,
i.e. we always have a formal HPS. It remains to prove that
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(\frac{\delta^{(n)}(0)}{n!}\right)_{{\rm c}},0\right)=\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
:
for all
z=[z_{\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}

\sum_{n=0}^{N}\frac{\delta^{(n)}(0)}{n!}z^{n}=\left[\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-2}\sum_{n=0}^{N_{\varepsilon}}\frac{\mu^{(n)}(0)}{n!}\left(\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-1}z_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n}\right]=\delta(z)-\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}^{-2}\left[\mu^{(N_{\varepsilon}+1)}(\hat{z}_{\varepsilon})\frac{z_{\varepsilon}^{N_{\varepsilon}+1}}{(N_{\varepsilon}+1)!}\right]

where the existence of
\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}\in B_{\left|z_{\varepsilon}\right|}(0)

is derived from Taylor’s formula of
\mu
and
\mu^{(N_{\varepsilon}+1)}(\hat{z}_{\varepsilon})\frac{z_{\varepsilon}^{N_{\varepsilon}+1}}{(N_{\varepsilon}+1)!}

is the Lagrange remainder. We obtain

\left|\sum_{n=0}^{N}\frac{\delta^{(n)}(0)}{n!}z^{n}-\delta(z)\right|\leq\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}^{-2}\left|\mu^{(N+1)}(\hat{z})\right|\frac{|z|^{N+1}}{\left(N+1\right)!}.

Using Stirling’s approximation, we have
\frac{|z|^{N+1}}{\left(N+1\right)!}\leq 2\left(\frac{|z|e}{N}\right)^{N}\leq\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}^{N}
,
for all
N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
such that
N>|z|e\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}^{-1}
. Since
\lim_{N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}}\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}^{N}=0
,
this proves the claim.

When we say that a HPS
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}(z-c)^{n}
is
convergent
,
we already assume that its coefficients are correctly chosen and that
the point
z
is in the set of convergence, as stated in the following:

Definition 37
.

We say that
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}(z-c)^{n}

is a
convergent
HPS, if

(i)

(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm c}}
are coefficients for
HPS;

(ii)

z\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left((a_{n})_{{\rm c}},c\right)
.

We now study absolute convergence of HPS, and sharply boundedness
of the summands of a HPS. We first show that the hypersequence
\left(a_{n}\left(z-c\right)^{n}\right)_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}}

of the terms of a HPS is sharply bounded:

Lemma 38
.

Let
(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm c}}

and
c\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
.

(i)

If
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}(z-c)^{n}
is a
convergent HPS, then

\exists K\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\;\forall n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}:\ |a_{n}(z-c)^{n}|<K.

(ii)

For all representatives
(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}=\left[a_{n\varepsilon}\right]_{{\rm c}}
and

[c_{\varepsilon}]=c
, there exists
\delta\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{>0}
such that
for all
z=[z_{\varepsilon}]\in B_{\delta}(c)
, we have

\exists K=[K_{\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{>0}\,\forall^{0}\varepsilon\,\forall n\in\mathbb{N}:\ \left|a_{n\varepsilon}(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right|<K_{\varepsilon}.

(2.31)

We say that
(a_{n}(z-c)^{n})_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}}
is
eventually
bounded
(in
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}{}_{{\rm c}}
) if there exist representatives

(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}=\left[a_{n\varepsilon}\right]_{{\rm c}}
,
[z_{\varepsilon}]=z
,
and
[c_{\varepsilon}]=c
such that (
2.31
) holds.

Proof.

(i)
: We recall that because of the definition of
formal HPS Def.
25
, the term
a_{n}(z-c)^{n}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}

is well-defined for all
n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
. Set
\hat{z}:=z-c
, i.e. without
loss of generality we can assume
c=0
. Since
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}\hat{z}^{n}

converges, from Lem.
18
, we have

\exists N\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,\forall n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}:|a_{n}\hat{z}^{n}|<1.

(2.32)

Let us consider an arbitrary
n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
. We have either
n\geq N

or
n<_{L}N
for some
L\subseteq_{0}I
. In the latter case,
|a_{n}\hat{z}^{n}|\leq_{L}s:=\sum_{n=0}^{N-1}|a_{n}\hat{z}^{n}|<\max\left(s+1,1\right)=:K
.
From this and (
38
), the claim follows.

(ii)
: Since
(a_{n})_{c}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{c}
,
we have
\forall^{0}\varepsilon\forall n\in\mathbb{N}:|a_{n\varepsilon}|\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-nQ-R}
.
Therefore, for
\delta:=\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}^{Q}
, we have
\left|a_{n\varepsilon}(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right|\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-nQ-R}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{nQ}=\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-R}=:K
.
∎

Note that the adjective
eventually
in
(ii)

above means that only for
\varepsilon\leq\varepsilon_{0}
(
\varepsilon_{0}
depending
on
(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}=\left[a_{n\varepsilon}\right]_{{\rm c}}
,
[z_{\varepsilon}]=z
,
and
[c_{\varepsilon}]=c
) the sequence
n\in\mathbb{N}\mapsto a_{n\varepsilon}(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\in\mathbb{C}

is bounded.
