Unfortunately, property (
2.31
) does not hold
for all points
z\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(a_{n}\right)_{{\rm c}},c\right)
.
For example, consider in Example
36
the Dirac delta
at
c=0
and
|z|\leq\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\hbox{\rm d}\rho}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}\rho$}}^{2}
: we have
\left|\frac{\delta_{\varepsilon}^{(n)}(0)}{n!}z_{\varepsilon}^{n}\right|=\left|\frac{\mu^{(n)}(0)}{n!}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-2n-2}z_{\varepsilon}^{n}\right|\leq\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-2}

for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
such that
\left|\frac{\mu^{(n)}(0)}{n!}\right|\leq 1
.
Therefore for this
z
,
\left(\frac{\delta^{(n)}(0)}{n!}z^{n}\right)_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}}

is eventually bounded in
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm c}}
. However, if
|z|\gg 0
,
i.e.
|z|\geq s\in\mathbb{R}_{>0}
, then
\left|\frac{\delta_{\varepsilon}^{(n)}(0)}{n!}z_{\varepsilon}^{n}\right|=\left|\frac{\mu^{(n)}(0)}{n!}\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-2n-2}z_{\varepsilon}^{n}\right|\geq\left|\frac{\mu^{(n)}(0)}{n!}\right|\rho_{\varepsilon}^{-2n-2}s^{n}

and hence condition (
2.31
) does not hold for
any
[K_{\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}
.

On the other hand, Example
34
of geometric hyperseries
and Example
35
of exponential HPS are all eventually
bounded at
c=0
and for all finite number
z
. This follows from
the property that, for
\varepsilon
small, the series
\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}\left|a_{n\varepsilon}\left(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon}\right)^{n}\right|=:K_{\varepsilon}

of absolute values terms converges to a
\rho
-moderate net.

The question of whether the convergence set is a sharply open set
remains an unsolved problem. Notably, the case of exponential HPS
(see Example
35
) demonstrates that the convergence
set is not, in general, a disk. A partial solution is to consider
the interior of
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(a_{n}\right)_{{\rm c}},c\right)
,
which is always not empty because of Lem.
38

and of Thm.
39
below. Another result
going in this direction is the following theorem, where interior points
of the set of convergence are related to points where we have eventual
boundedness.

Theorem 39
.

Let
(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}=[a_{n\varepsilon}]_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm c}}
,

c=[c_{\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
, and
\hat{z}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(a_{n}\right)_{{\rm c}},c\right)
.
If
\left(a_{n}(\hat{z}-c)^{n}\right)_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}}
is eventually
bounded, then for
all
z=[z_{\varepsilon}]\in B_{\left|\hat{z}-c\right|}(c)

we have

(i)

The HPS
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}(z-c)^{n}

converges absolutely and
\left(a_{n}(z-c)^{n}\right)_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}}

is eventually bounded. Moreover,

\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}(z-c)^{n}=\left[\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}a_{n\varepsilon}(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}};

(ii)

For all functionally compact set
K\Subset_{\text{\emph{f}}}\overline{B_{|\hat{z}-c|}(c)}
,

the HPS
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}(z-c)^{n}
converges uniformly
on
K
.

(iii)

The point
z
is a sharply interior
point of
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(\left(a_{n}\right)_{{\rm c}},c\right)
.

Proof.

Without loss of generality we can assume
c=0
.

(i)
: We have either
\hat{z}=_{L}0
or

\left|\hat{z}\right|>0
for some
L\subseteq_{0}I
. The first case
is actually impossible because
0\leq|z|<|\hat{z}|=_{L}0
. We can
hence work only in the latter case
|\hat{z}|>0
. From Lemma
38
(ii)
,
we have
\forall^{0}\varepsilon\,\forall n\in\mathbb{N}:|a_{n\varepsilon}\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}^{n}|\leq K_{\varepsilon}
.
Setting
h:=\left|\frac{z}{\hat{z}}\right|
, we have
h<1
, because

z\in B_{|\hat{z}|}(0)
, and

\forall^{0}\varepsilon\,\forall n\in\mathbb{N}:|a_{n\varepsilon}z_{\varepsilon}^{n}|=|a_{n\varepsilon}\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}^{n}|\cdot\left|\frac{z}{\hat{z}}\right|^{n}<K_{\varepsilon}h_{\varepsilon}^{n}.

(2.33)

Thereby,
\sum_{n=N}^{M}|a_{n}z^{n}|\leq\sum_{n=N}^{M}Kh^{n}
for
all
N
,
M\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
. By the direct comparison test (see Thm.
22
),
the HPS
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}z^{n}
converges absolutely because

\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}Kh^{n}
converges since
h<1
. Moreover, from
(
2.33
), it follows that
\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}|a_{n\varepsilon}z_{\varepsilon}^{n}|=:R_{\varepsilon}

converges and is
\rho
-moderate. This implies condition (
2.31
).

(ii)
: From
[
6
, Thm. 74]
,
we have that pointwise convergence implies uniform convergence on
functionally compact sets.

(iii)
: From the assumptions,
z\in B_{\left|\hat{z}\right|}(0)

and
\left|\hat{z}\right|<{\rm rad}\left(a_{n}\right)_{{\rm c}}
,
conditions Def.
31
(i)

and Def.
31
(iii)
follow. Note
that Def.
31
(ii)
can be
proved as above from (
2.33
). Finally, if

\left[\tilde{z}_{\varepsilon}\right]=z
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{{\rm d}}{{\rm d}z}\left(\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}a_{n\varepsilon}z^{n}\right)_{z=\tilde{z}_{\varepsilon}}\right|

\displaystyle=\left|\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}na_{n\varepsilon}\tilde{z}_{\varepsilon}^{n-1}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}\left|a_{n\varepsilon}\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}^{n-1}\right|\cdot n\left|\frac{\tilde{z}_{\varepsilon}}{\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}}\right|^{n-1}

\displaystyle\leq K_{\varepsilon}\left|\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}\right|\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}n\left|\frac{\tilde{z}_{\varepsilon}}{\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}}\right|^{n-1}\in\mathbb{R}_{\rho},

where we used Lem.
38
, and hence Def.
31
(iv)
.
∎

Based on this theory of generalized complex HPS, we can finally introduce
the notion of generalized complex analytic functions (GCAF) and connect
it with GHF.

3.
Generalized complex analytic functions

For the theory of GHF and their path integrals, we refer to
[
9
,
10
]
.

A direct consequence of Def.
31
is the following:

Theorem 40
.

Let
[a_{n\varepsilon}]_{{\rm c}}=(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm c}}

(see Def.
27
) and
c=[c_{\varepsilon}]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
.
Let
U:=\mathrm{int}\left(\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left((a_{n})_{{\rm c}},c\right)\right)

be the sharply interior of the set of convergence. Set

f(z):=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.68887pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}(z-c)^{n}=\left[\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}a_{n\varepsilon}(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right]=:[v_{\varepsilon}(z_{\varepsilon})],

for all
z=[z_{\varepsilon}]\in U
. Then
f\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\mathcal{GH}\left(U\right)
is a
GHF defined by
(v_{\varepsilon})
.

Proof.

Since
z\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left((a_{n})_{{\rm c}},c\right)
, then

|z-c|<\text{rad}(a_{n})_{{\rm c}}
,
\left[a_{n\varepsilon}(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right]_{{\rm s}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm s}}\left\llbracket z-c\right\rrbracket
,

\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}(z-c)^{n}=\left[\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}a_{n\varepsilon}(z_{\varepsilon}-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right]\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}},

and for all representatives
[\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}]=z
we have

\left(\frac{\text{d}}{\text{d}z}\left(\sum_{n=0}^{+\infty}a_{n\varepsilon}(z-c_{\varepsilon})^{n}\right)_{z=\hat{z}_{\varepsilon}}\right)\in\mathbb{C}_{\rho}.

Therefore, the conclusion directly follows from
[
9
, Thm. 26]
.
∎

Conversely, we conjecture that our GHF are generalized complex analytic
functions, as defined below:

Definition 41
.

Let
U\subseteq\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
be a simply connected and sharply open
set. We say that
f:U\longrightarrow\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
is a
generalized complex
analytic function
(GCAF) on
U
, if for all
c\in U
, we can find

s\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{>0}
,
\left(a_{n}\right)_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}_{{\rm c}}

such that

(i)

B_{s}(c)\subseteq U\cap\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left((a_{n})_{{\rm c}},c\right)
,

(ii)

f(z)=\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}}a_{n}\left(z-c\right)^{n}

for all
z\in B_{s}(c)
.

Moreover, we say that
f:\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}\longrightarrow\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
is an
generalized
entire function
if we can find
c\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
and
\left(a_{n}\right)_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{{\rm c}}

such that

(iii)

\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}=\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left((a_{n})_{{\rm c}},c\right)
,

(iv)

f(z)=\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}}a_{n}\left(z-c\right)^{n}

for all
z\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
.

We also say that
f
is
generalized entire at

c
if
(iii)

and
(iv)
hold.

Example
36
shows that Dirac
\delta

is entire at
0
, but it is not at any
c\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
such
that
|z|\gg 0
.

Clearly, if
\left(a_{n}\right)_{{\rm c}}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{{\rm c}}
,

c\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
, and we set
f(z):=\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}}a_{n}\left(z-c\right)^{n}

then
f
is generalized complex analytic on the interior of the set
of convergence
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left((a_{n})_{{\rm c}},c\right)
,
as proved in Thm.
40
. Vice versa, as expected,
our GHF are generalized analytic. For the proof, we follow the classical
strategy, leveraging our generalized framework to bypass the need
for explicit
\varepsilon
-level calculations.

Theorem 42

(Goursat)
.

Suppose
U\subseteq\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
is a simply
connected and sharply open set. If
f:U\longrightarrow\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
is a generalized
holomorphic function then
f
is generalized analytic on
U
.

Proof.

Let
z_{0}=[z_{0\varepsilon}]\in U
and
f=\left[f_{\varepsilon}(-)\right]
. Then,
we can choose
R\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{>0}
such that
\overline{B_{R}(z_{0})}\subset U
.
Let
0<s<R
. Set
\gamma_{R}(t):=z_{0}+R\exp(2\pi it)
for all
t\in[0,1]
,
so that
\gamma_{R}
is closed, simple, and positively oriented,
and the region bounded by
\gamma_{R}
is
R_{\gamma_{R}}\subset U
.
Note that for all
z\in B_{s}(z_{0})
, by Cauchy integral formula
we have

f(z)=\frac{1}{2\pi i}\int_{\gamma_{R}}\frac{f(w)}{w-z}\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}w}{\hbox{\rm d}w}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}w$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}w$}}.

For all
w\in\gamma_{R}
, we have
R=|w-z_{0}|>0
,
\left|\frac{z-z_{0}}{w-z_{0}}\right|<\frac{s}{R}<1

and hence the geometric hyperseries
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\rho\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\left(\frac{z-z_{0}}{w-z_{0}}\right)^{n}=\frac{1}{1-\left|\frac{z-z_{0}}{w-z_{0}}\right|}

and

\displaystyle\frac{1}{w-z}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{w-z_{0}-(z-z_{0})}=\frac{1}{w-z_{0}}\left(\frac{1}{1-\left|\frac{z-z_{0}}{w-z_{0}}\right|}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{w-z_{0}}\left(\phantom{|}\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\left(\frac{z-z_{0}}{w-z_{0}}\right)^{n}\right).

So, we have

\frac{f(w)}{w-z}=\frac{f(w)}{w-z_{0}}\left(\phantom{|}\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\left(\frac{z-z_{0}}{w-z_{0}}\right)^{n}\right)=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}f(w)\frac{\left(z-z_{0}\right)^{n}}{\left(w-z_{0}\right)^{n+1}}

and according to convergence theorem on functionally compact sets

[
6
, Thm. 75]
, pointwise convergence implies uniform
convergence on functionally compact set, and hence, we can exchange
integration and hyperseries to have

\displaystyle f(z)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\pi i}\int_{\gamma_{R}}\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}f(w)\frac{\left(z-z_{0}\right)^{n}}{\left(w-z_{0}\right)^{n+1}}\,\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}w}{\hbox{\rm d}w}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}w$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}w$}}

\displaystyle=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\left(\frac{1}{2\pi i}\int_{\gamma_{R}}\frac{f(w)}{\left(w-z_{0}\right)^{n+1}}\,\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}w}{\hbox{\rm d}w}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}w$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}w$}}\right)\left(z-z_{0}\right)^{n}.

∎

Classical properties of the interplay between holomorphic and analytic
functions are Liouville’s theorem, the identity principle, and Paley-Wiener
theorem. We first prove Liouville’s theorem:

Theorem 43

(Liouville)
.

Every bounded entire
f\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\mathcal{GH}(\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}})
,
i.e. for which there exists
M\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{>0}
such that
|f(z)|<M

for all
z\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
, is constant.

Proof.

Without loss of generality, we can assume that
f
is entire at
c=0
.
By Goursat’s theorem
42
, for all
z\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}

we have

f(z)=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}a_{n}z^{n}\;\text{where}\;a_{n}=\frac{f^{(n)}(0)}{n!}=\frac{1}{2\pi i}\int_{\gamma_{r}}\frac{f(w)}{w^{n+1}}\,\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}w}{\hbox{\rm d}w}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}w$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}w$}}

where
\gamma_{r}
is any circle centered at
0
of radius
r\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{>0}

since
f
is entire in
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle 0\leq|a_{n}|=\left|\frac{1}{2\pi i}\int_{\gamma_{r}}\frac{f(w)}{w^{n+1}}\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}w}{\hbox{\rm d}w}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}w$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}w$}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\gamma_{r}}\frac{|f(w)|}{|w|^{n+1}}\,\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}w}{\hbox{\rm d}w}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}w$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}w$}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{2\pi}\int_{\gamma_{r}}\frac{M}{r^{n+1}}\mathchoice{\hbox{\rm d}w}{\hbox{\rm d}w}{\scalebox{0.75}{$\hbox{\rm d}w$}}{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hbox{\rm d}w$}}

\displaystyle=\frac{M}{2\pi r^{n+1}}2\pi r

\displaystyle=\frac{M}{r^{n}}.

Letting
r\to+\infty
in the sharp topology gives
|a_{n}|=0
for
all
n\in\mathbb{N}
. Thus,
f(z)=a_{0}
.
∎

Before we study the identity theorem for generalized holomorphic functions,
we extend one important properties of a holomorphic function i.e. its
zeroes are isolated (assuming the function is not identically zero):

Theorem 44
.

Let
U\subseteq\mathbb{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}}
be a sharply
open set,
z_{0}\in U
, and
f\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\mathcal{GH}(U)
. If

f(z_{0})=0,\;\exists n\in\mathbb{N}_{>0}:f^{(n)}(z_{0})\neq 0,

(3.1)

then there exist
L=L(z_{0})\subseteq_{0}I
and
r\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{>0}

such that

\forall z\in B_{r}(z_{0}):|z-z_{0}|>0\implies f(z)\mid_{L}\;\text{is invertible.}

(3.2)

Proof.

Since
f\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\mathcal{GH}(U)
, we have

\exists s\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{>0}\,\forall z\in B_{s}(z_{0}):f(z)=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\frac{f^{(n)}(z_{0})}{n!}(z-z_{0})^{n}.

(3.3)

From (
3.1
), there exists
m:=\min\left\{n\in\mathbb{N}_{>0}:f^{(n)}(z_{0})\neq 0\right\}>0
,
and
f^{(m)}(z_{0})\neq 0
. On the other hand, from (
3.3
),
we have

f(z)=(z-z_{0})^{m}\cdot\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}_{\geq m}}\frac{f^{(n)}(z_{0})}{n!}(z-z_{0})^{n-m}=:(z-z_{0})^{m}\cdot g(z),

with
g(z_{0})=f^{(m)}(z_{0})\neq 0
. This means
\exists L\subseteq_{0}I:g(z_{0})\mid_{L}

is invertible, hence
\left|g(z_{0})\right|\mid_{L}>0
. Since
g(-):B_{s}(z_{0})\longrightarrow\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}

is sharply continuous, then
\exists r\in(0,s)

\forall z\in B_{r}(z_{0}):\left|g(z)\right|\mid_{L}>0
.
Therefore:

\forall z\in B_{r}(z_{0}):|z-z_{0}|>0\implies|f(z)|=|z-z_{0}|\cdot|g(z)|,

which proves our claim:
f(z)\mid_{L}
is invertible.
∎

Note that (
3.1
) represents the property of being non-zero.
Moreover, the conclusion (
3.2
) states that
z_{0}

is isolated, at least on a suitable subpoint.

As we already discussed in
[
9
, Sec. 4]
, since
\delta

is a GHF, and hence is a generalized analytic function, in general
the identity principle does not hold for GHF. The following theorem
shows that the identity principle does not hold in our framework because
of two motivations: we are in a non-Archimedean setting and hence
the set of all the infinitesimals is a clopen set (see
[
9
, Thm. 43]
),
and because analytic continuation from a point
z_{0}
to another

z
cannot, in general, be reversed for GHF:

Theorem 45

(Identity principle)
.

Let
U\subseteq\mathbb{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}}
be a
sharply open set,
(z_{k})_{k\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}}
be a hypersequence of points
in
U
such that
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-0.42224pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-0.42224pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-0.42224pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-0.42224pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\lim_{k\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{k\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{k\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{k\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}z_{k}=z_{0}\in U
,
\left|z_{k}-z_{0}\right|>0

for all
k\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
, and
f\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\mathcal{GH}(U)
such that
f(z_{k})=0

for all
k\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
. Assume that
f

can be continued

z_{0}\longrightarrow z
(we read it: “from
z_{0}
to z”) for all
z\in U
,
i.e. there exist
N\in\mathbb{N}
,
c_{1},c_{2},\dots,c_{N}\in U
and
r_{1},r_{2},\dots,r_{N}\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{>0}

such that:

(i)

c_{1}=z_{0}
,
c_{N}=z
,

(ii)

c_{k+1}\in B_{r_{k}}(c_{k})
for all
k=1,\dots,N-1
,

(iii)

B_{r_{k}}(c_{k})\subseteq\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(f,c_{k}\right)

for all
k=1,\dots,N
.

Then,

f(z)=0\quad\forall z\in U.

Proof.

We consider two cases:

Case I: If
U\subseteq\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(f,z_{0}\right)
,
then

f(z)=\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-1.59442pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\sum}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\sum_{n\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}\frac{f^{(n)}(z_{0})}{n!}(z-z_{0})^{n}\quad\forall z\in U.

By contradiction, assume
\exists n\in\mathbb{N}_{>0}:f^{(n)}(z_{0})\neq 0
.
As we did in the proof of Thm.
44
, there exists
m:=\min\left\{n\in\mathbb{N}_{>0}:f^{(n)}(z_{0})\neq 0\right\}>0
,
which implies
f^{(m)}(z_{0})\neq 0
, and there exist
L=L(z_{0})\subseteq_{0}I
,
a continuous function
g
, and
r\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}_{>0}
such that

\forall z\in B_{r}(z_{0}):|z-z_{0}|>0\implies\left|f(z)\right|\mid_{L}=|z-z_{0}|\cdot|g(z)|\mid_{L}>0

(3.4)

with
g(z_{0})=f^{(m)}(z_{0})\neq 0
. However, the assumption
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\lim_{k\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{k\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{k\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{k\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}z_{k}=z_{0}\in U
,
yields the existence of
K\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}
such that
z_{K}\in B_{r}(z_{0})

and
|z_{K}-z_{0}|>0
. This means
f(z_{K})=0
, which implies
g(z_{K})=0

and hence also
|g(z_{K})|\mid_{L}=0
, which is a contradiction with
(
3.4
).

Case II: Let
z\in U
and only in this case assume that

f
can be extended
z_{0}\longrightarrow z
. From Case I, we have
f\mid_{B_{r_{0}}(c_{0})}=0

becausee
B_{r_{0}}(c_{0})\subseteq\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.6}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.56}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.47}{$$}}}{{\vphantom{\text{\rm c}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}_{\scalebox{0.35}{$$}}}\text{\rm conv}\left(f,c_{0}\right)
.
Since
c_{1}\in B_{r_{0}}(c_{0})
, there exists
(z_{1k})_{k\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}}

sequence of
B_{r_{0}}(c_{0})
converging to
c_{1}
and such that

\left|z_{1k}-c_{1}\right|>0
. Since
\mathchoice{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\raisebox{-0.39864pt}{$\hskip-5.49028pt$}$}}}\lim_{k\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{k\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{k\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}{\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}{{\vphantom{\lim}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\hskip-2.04861pt$}}}\lim_{k\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{N}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{N}}\,}}z_{1k}=c_{1}
,

f(z_{1k})=0
for all
k\in\mathbb{N}
, we have
f\mid_{B_{r_{1}}(c_{1})}=0
.
Since we only have a finite number of
c_{k}
, by induction, we have

f\mid_{B_{r_{N}}(c_{N})}=0
and this proves our claim because
c_{N}=z
.
∎

Remark 46
.

(i)

The Dirac
\delta
can be extended
0\longrightarrow 1
, but it cannot be
1\longrightarrow 0
.
Note that
\delta\mid_{B_{r}(0)}\neq 0
whereas
\delta\mid_{B_{s}(1)}=0
,
so
(ii)
cannot hold. Similarly, the Heaviside function
on
\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}
, see
[
10
]
, cannot be continued
1\longrightarrow 0

but it can be continued
0\longrightarrow 1
.

(ii)

The continuation assumption is invoked exclusively in Case II. Since
we used induction, it is important that in this case
N\in\mathbb{N}
is
a standard natural number.

4.
A Paley-Wiener type theorem for the hyperfinite Fourier transform

In this last section, we generalize a Paley-Wiener type theorem to
the framework of GSF and GHF. The classical Paley-Wiener theorems
make use of the holomorphic Fourier transform on classes of square-integrable
functions supported on the real line, and relates decay properties
of a function or distribution at infinity (such as being compactly
supported) with analyticity of its Fourier transform (FT). On the
contrary to the classical setting, we will see that the hyperfinite
Fourier transform (HFT) of
any
GSF
f
is always a GHF, without
assuming any compactness support of
f
. Moreover, the HFT is always
an entire GHF if the domain of integration is of logarithmic type.

The basic idea to define a very general FT for GSF is the following,
see
[
8
]
: Since GSF are based on a non-Archimedean ring of
scalars, we can consider a positive
infinite
generalized number

h
and define the FT with the usual formula, but integrating over
the interval
[-h,h]
. We know that, although
h
is an infinite
number (hence,
[-h,h]\supseteq\mathbb{R}
!), this interval behaves like
a compact set for GSF, so that, e.g., on these integration domains
we always have an extreme value theorem and integrals always exist.
Clearly, this leads to a FT, called
hyperfinite
FT, that depends
on the parameter
h
, but, on the other hand, where we can transform

all
the GSF defined on this interval, and these include all
tempered Schwartz distributions, all tempered Colombeau GF, but also
a large class of non-tempered GF, such as the exponential functions,
or non-linear examples like
\delta^{a}\circ\delta^{b}
,
\delta^{a}\circ H^{b}
,

a
,
b\in\mathbb{N}
, etc. Not all the properties of the classical FT remain
unchanged for this more general transform, but the final formalism
still retains the useful properties of the FT in dealing with differential
equations. Even more, the new formula for the transform of derivatives
leads us to include also exponential solutions in the Fourier method
applied to the simplest ODE:
y^{\prime}=y
,
y(0)=1
, see
[
8
]
.

We start by first recalling the notion of compactly supported GSF,
because we will use it to fully develop the example of the HFT of
Dirac delta. In the generalized functions framework, compactly supported
GSF were introduced in
[
8
]
for an arbitrary gauge, and in

[
5
]
for the gauge
\rho_{\varepsilon}=\varepsilon
.

Definition 47
.

Assume that
X\subseteq\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}

and
f\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\mathcal{GC}^{\infty}\left(X,\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{C}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{C}}\right)
, then

(i)

{\rm supp}\left(f\right):=\overline{\left\{x\in X\mid\left|f(x)\right|>0\right\}}
,
where
\overline{\left(\cdot\right)}
denotes the relative closure
in
X
with respect to the sharp topology, is called
the
support of

f
. Note that
{\rm supp}\left(f\right)
is clearly
always sharply closed. However, in general it is not an internal set,
so it is not a functionally compact set.

(ii)

For
A\subseteq\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}
, we call the set

{\rm ext}\left(A\right):=\left\{x\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}\mid\forall a\in A:\left|x-a\right|>0\right\}

the strong exterior of
A
. For example, using the generalized Jordan
curve theorem
[
10
, Thm. 40]
, it is easy to prove that if

\gamma\in\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathcal{G}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\mathcal{GC}^{1}([a,b])
is closed, simple and positively oriented
path, then
{\rm ext}\left(\overline{I}_{\gamma}\right)=E_{\gamma}
.

(iii)

Let
H\Subset_{{\rm f}}\mathchoice{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.6}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.56}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.47}{$\rho$}}}{{\vphantom{\mathbb{R}}}{}^{\scalebox{0.35}{$\rho$}}}\widetilde{\mathbb{R}}

be a functionally compact set, we say that
f
is
compactly
supported in

H
, and write
